- [MED] 可恢复 stage 集合 recoverable_stages 在 orchestrator.py 的 _watchdog_coroutine 和 orchestrator_session.py 的 _startup_recovery 里被硬编码了两份（且与 STAGE_GATE_ALLOWLIST 的语义重叠），任一处新增/改名 stage 漏改另一处即静默失效（例如新加一个 stage 进 STAGE_ORDER 但 watchdog 不认 → 该 stage 卡住永不触发 watchdog）。 — *证据：web/core/orchestrator_session.py:92-93 (recoverable_stages 字面量); web/core/orchestrator.py:567-568 (同名字面量); web/core/orchestrator_session.py:143-144 (第三处同集合)*
- [MED] _decide_strategy 没有任何 'genesis'（从零生成）出口，也无对'同一代反复 prepare 失败'的代级熔断。定义的 MAX_GENESIS_RETRIES=3 常量在调度器里从未被消费（只在 agent_review crossover 路径用到 MAX_CROSSOVER_RETRIES）。当连续多代退化、所有 crossover/master 路径都失败时，系统没有'回滚到早期稳定版本'或'强制 genesis'的机械逃生，只能依赖 LLM 自觉 abandon_generation。 — *证据：web/core/generation_scheduler.py:33 (strategy 注释仅 'master'|'crossover'); web/core/evolution_infra.py:74 (MAX_GENESIS_RETRIES=3 定义但无 'genesis' 字符串产出路径); web/core/agent_review.py:308 (仅 MAX_CROSSOVER_RETRIES 被消费)*
- [LOW] cost cap (MAX_GEN_COST=$5) 仅做可观测性（log warning + system event），没有任何强制中断。orchestrator.py:200-217 注释自己承认 'CYCLE_TIMEOUT (60min) is the hard backstop'，即 cost runaway 仍会烧满一小时才被超时杀掉，cap 形同虚设。 — *证据：web/core/orchestrator.py:200-217 (仅 log warning + log_system_event，未 raise/中断 _stream_response); web/core/orchestrator.py:256 (CYCLE_TIMEOUT 仍是唯一硬超时)*
- [MED] watchdog 恢复路径构造 GenerationContext 时，strategy 仅按 'parent2_v 存在则 crossover 否则 master' 推断 (orchestrator.py:738, 700)，丢失了 prepare_generation 里 _decide_strategy 的退化诊断 override 与 diversity 注入上下文（stagnation_info/perf 等字段全部为空）。恢复后的代是'裸'策略，缺少驱动该代规划的分析数据，可能让恢复代产出的 Master plan 质量明显低于正常代。 — *证据：web/core/orchestrator.py:734-747 (resume 分支读 ckpt 但不重算 source_v/strategy 校验); web/core/generation_scheduler.py:255 (next_v=current_v+1 用 find_current_v() 而非 ckpt.next_v)*
- [LOW] _run_one_cycle 的 except Exception 通用分支里，cycle_failed=True 后若 is_infra 则清 session 返回 -0.5，否则返回 -1.0；但 orchestrator_loop 对 cost<0 的处理把 -1.0 一律当作 'auth error 300s backoff' (orchestrator.py:838-849)，对真正非 auth 的业务异常（如 _build_context 抛 KeyError）也套用 300s 长 backoff，过度保守地拉慢了可快速重试的失败。 — *证据：web/core/orchestrator.py:874-878 (注释仅提到'保留 checkpoint 让 startup recovery 评估'，但 orchestrator_session.json 未在 except Exception 路径显式保留，仅 CancelledError 分支注释明确保留)*
- [LOW] exploitability probe 用裸 threading.Thread + _probe_running Event 做单飞 (generation_scheduler.py:714-757)，但 _probe_running.clear() 只在 worker 的 finally 里，若 worker 线程被进程强杀（OOM/SIGKILL）Event 永不清除，后续所有代的探针都会被 single_flight_skip 永久跳过，且无进程级 reset。 — *证据：web/core/generation_scheduler.py:754-757 (threading.Thread 裸起); web/core/orchestrator.py:880-888 (finally 只 cancel _watchdog_task，未 join 探针线程); 进程被 SIGKILL 时探针线程无 cleanup*
- [MED] prepare_generation 里 H2H 异常检测用固定阈值 games>=20 且 |wr-0.5|>0.15 (generation_scheduler.py:225) 注入 stagnation_text，但 wr 来自 head_to_head.json 的累积值，没有考虑对手是否已被 reap 进 graveyard——对已淘汰对手的旧胜率异常会持续误导 Master，是历史'评估噪声误导选择'的一个潜在来源。 — *证据：web/core/generation_scheduler.py:225 (anomalies 只判 games>=20 且 |wr-0.5|>0.15)；prepare_generation 注入字段为 stagnation_text += anomaly_text (242)，与 trend/stagnation 同一字段混入*
- [LOW] _run_master_analysis 的审计 retry 循环里，audit_attempt 持久化用 write_pipeline_checkpoint(next_v, source_v, 'master_planned', master_plan=data, audit_attempt=...) (tool_planning.py:508)，但此处的 merge 守卫是 existing.next_v==next_v 且 source_v==source_v 才合并——若 prepare_next_gen 写入的 next_v 与本次不同（跨代边界），audit_attempt 会从 None 重新开始，导致 crash-resume 时审计预算被偷偷重置（注释 463-466 自己点名了这个坑但仍依赖 int(None or 0) 的运行时兜底，没有在写入侧保证非 None）。 — *证据：web/core/tool_planning.py:467 (_audit_attempt=int(_ckpt0.get('audit_attempt') or 0)); web/core/tool_planning.py:508-509 (retry 时 write audit_attempt)*

**Prompt 问题**：

- `web/core/prompts/orchestrator.md`：gate_requirements 仍要求 'run_critic returned approved: true' 作为 commit 前置，且 retry_rules 写 'Critic score is ADVISORY ONLY'，但 critic 在代码层已恒 approved=True，这条 gate 在 commit_bot 与 run_precommit_eval 中被机械校验却是空转（critic 永远 approved）。prompt 没有说明 critic gate 实际等价于'只要调用过 run_critic'，读者/未来维护者会误以为 critic 分数仍参与拦截。 → 建议：在 <gate_requirements> 与 <retry_rules> 里明确：critic gate 仅要求'已被调用过'（run_critic 必返回 approved=true），真正回归门是 precommit；并删除 'run_critic returned approved: true' 作为 commit 前置的措辞，改为 'run_critic 已被调用'。
- `web/core/prompts/orchestrator.md`：retry_rules 让 LLM 自维护 'intra_gen_attempts' 局部计数器，但真实计数器是 checkpoint 的 generation_attempt + worker_failure_count（execute_workers:927 用 generation_attempt≥1 强制重规划，:909 用 worker_failure_count≥6 circuit break）。LLM 局部计数器会被 compaction/PreCompact 清掉且与 checkpoint 不同步，prompt 把决策权交给一个不可靠的局部变量。 → 建议：去掉让 LLM 自数 intra_gen_attempts 的指令，改为告知 LLM：tool 返回的 generation_attempt/circuit_breaker/require_new_plan 字段才是权威，严格按这些字段的 directive 行动，不要自己计数。
- `web/core/prompts/orchestrator.md`：context 工具列表 (orchestrator_context.py:160) 把 run_critic 签名写成 (version, source_v, plan, reviewer_feedback, force_advance)，但 critic 已 advisory，force_advance 在 advisory 模式下无实际拦截语义（commit_bot 只在 critic.approved!=True 且 force_advanced==True 时放行，而 approved 恒为 True 永远不进该分支）。把 force_advance 暴露给 LLM 是一个无作用的逃生舱，可能诱导 LLM 滥用。 → 建议：从 run_critic 工具签名与 context 工具列表中移除 force_advance（或标注 deprecated/ignored），避免 LLM 误以为可以强制通过 critic。
- `web/core/prompts/master_prompt.md`：plateau_protocol 鼓励'纯结构探索'与'Aggressive parameter exploration (test extreme values)'，但 _validate_master_plan 的 HARD gate 要求 Tuner 只能改 constants.py 且 Architect/Tuner 不能共享文件，而多数 plateau 代只有 1 个 worker (Architect)。prompt 没有把'极端参数探索需 Tuner worker + constants.py-only'这一硬约束说清楚，Master 容易产出违反 HARD gate 的 plan 被 reject 后浪费一次 run_master。 → 建议：在 <plateau_protocol> 的 'Aggressive parameter exploration' 条目后补一句：若走参数探索必须新增一个 role=Hyperparameter Tuner 且 target_files 仅 constants.py 的 worker，且不得与 Architect worker 共享任何文件。
- `web/core/prompts/master_prompt.md`：injected_context 列了 {bot_action_stats} 与 {exploitability_weaknesses}，但 prepare_generation 的 GenerationContext 并不携带这些（tool_planning.run_master 才从文件重算并注入 _run_master_analysis）。GenerationContext 只带 stagnation/match/perf/replay/battle_experience。prompt 的 {bot_action_stats}/{exploitability_weaknesses} 占位符在 agent_master 渲染路径里才存在，与 generation_scheduler 的字段名不一致，容易让人误以为这些数据由 scheduler 准备。 → 建议：在 prompt 顶部注明 bot_action_stats / exploitability_weaknesses 由 run_master MCP 工具从 results 文件即时读取并注入，不由 Phase 1 调度器提供，避免维护者误改 GenerationContext 字段。
- `web/core/prompts/critic_prompt.md`：scoring 表写 'Score >= 6 → approved: true. Score < 6 → approved: false'，但代码 (tool_gates.py:678) 已把 approved 恒置 True、critic 仅作 advisory。prompt 仍让 critic LLM 输出 approved 字段并暗示它决定通过与否，与代码实际行为矛盾——critic LLM 不知道自己的 approved 字段会被无条件覆盖。 → 建议：明确告诉 critic LLM：approved 字段仅供参考/遥测，不作为最终门；它的真实产出是 score + feedback + local_optima_warning，后者会注入下一代 worker prompt。删除或弱化 'Score >= 6 → approved: true' 的硬措辞。

### 经验/记忆/多样性层

**现状**：The "experience/memory/diversity" layer is split across two physically separate persistent memory stores, one diversity/selection policy in the daemon, one reap policy for the bot pool, and one mandatory-fix injection engine. They are not unified.

TWO MEMORY STORES:
1. experience_pool.md (web/core/experience_pool.md) — curated strategic lessons. 38 lines currently. Section-structured (OPPONENT_MODELING / POSTFLOP_STRATEGY / BLUFF_CALIBRATION / PARAMETER_TUNING / GENERAL / RECENT_LESSONS). Injected verbatim into the Master prompt (master_prompt.md:13) and worker prompt (worker_prompt.md) as authoritative guidance. Consolidated by LLM every 3 generations via experience_archivist._consolidate_experience_pool (triggered in generation_scheduler.py:586-618). trim_experience_pool (experience_pool.py:18) is the only mechanical decay: a hard line-count cap — if file > MAX_EXPERIENCE_LINES (120), keep the last KEEP_EXPERIENCE_LINES (100) lines, dropping the oldest. Called from tool_bot_management.py:217 (reap path) and re-exported via evolution_infra/evolution_core. There is no version/time/EV-based decay; only the consolidator LLM decides what to drop.

2. battle_experience.md (web/core/results/battle_experience.md) — 35 lines currently. Incrementally maintained by a daemon background thread (battle_experience.py). start_experience_thread is spawned inside the daemon ProcessPool loop (elo_daemon.py:578-584). The thread wakes every POLL_INTERVAL=20s, randomly samples up to TARGET_BATCH=6 unanalyzed matches, extracts per-match deterministic summaries in parallel (MAX_CONCURRENT_LLM=6, replay_analysis.summarize_replay_for_analysis), then does ONE cumulative LLM merge over the whole batch (_apply_batch_results → _run_llm_update) writing back atomically. The 2026-06-15 RC4 / batch-merge design explicitly fixed a parallel read-modify-write data-loss bug. Markers (.battle_analysis_progress.json) track fail_count: 0=done, 1-2=retryable transient, >=3=force-skip poison. Injected into Master prompt via {battle_experience} placeholder (master_prompt.md:134) and into GenerationContext (generation_scheduler.py:248-264). battle_experience.md has NO line cap and NO consolidator — it grows until the LLM self-trims to the prompt's 80-line instruction (battle_experience_update.md:14).

DIVERSITY / SELECTION:
- Match-pair selection in the daemon (elo_daemon.pick_matches, elo_daemon.py:198-229): score = UNDER_EVAL_WEIGHT(0.6)*under_eval + DIVERSITY_WEIGHT(0.4)*diversity*count_penalty + new_pair_bonus. under_eval favors pairs with few games; diversity = |rating_gap|/200 capped at 1; count_penalty decays pair score as its game count grows. Pairs shuffled before scoring to break alphabetical bias. 60/40 exploration-under-eval vs rating-diverse exploitation. New committed bots get a strong boost via priority_eval.json (front-of-queue).
- Bot pool cap: MAX_ACTIVE_BOTS=30 (evolution_infra.py:52). Reap via tool_bot_management._do_reap_weakest: only triggers when pool > 30. Candidates sorted by Glicko conservative rating r-2*rd (NOT H2H winrate despite the MCP doc string). Protection: rd>100 OR games<600 protected (sample-starved variance victims), protection relaxed only past hard cap 30+3. Triggered from generation_scheduler (3 sites) and tool_commit.

FIX INJECTION (fix_injection.py): a static registry MANDATORY_FIXES of 3 active Fix objects (BOT-001a wheel straight, BOT-002a re-raise min, BOT-004 TOTAL_HANDS=70) + 1 dead template (BOT-002b active=False). Each Fix = list of Patch (exact search→replace + idempotency guard). apply_known_fixes runs idempotent search-and-replace under fcntl.LOCK_EX. Applied at 4 sites: prepare_next_gen (tool_gates.py:370), worker retry (tool_planning.py:954), code_verification seed (code_verification.py:207), crossover agent (agent_review.py:315). Results logged via log_fix_application → system_log.

EXHAUSTED-DIRECTION GATE (the exploration/exploitation control):
- experience_pool.md entries can be tagged [POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED] by the consolidator LLM (experience_consolidator.md:26-37) when a lesson repeats 3+ generations with no gain.
- _extract_exhausted_block (agent_workers.py:56) splits EXHAUSTED entries into <forbidden_directions> (RECENT_LESSONS section = hard ban injected into worker) vs <advisory_directions> (other sections = caution). Tiering is per-section, consolidator guarantees RECENT_LESSONS only holds last ~3 gens.
- _extract_exhausted_keywords + _fuzzy_match_exhausted (tool_planning.py:566-700) does token-overlap matching of worker prompts against EXHAUSTED phrases; HARD gate (_validate_master_plan) requires >=1 direction-characteristic token (parameter/tuning/commitment) for a block, soft gate in execute_workers just warns.
- A tag-identity round-trip gate (experience_archivist._tag_identity) aborts the consolidator write if it would drop an EXHAUSTED tag class entirely, so consolidation cannot silently disable the gate.

NOVELTY MEASUREMENT: there is NO mechanical novelty metric anywhere in code. The only "novelty" signal is an LLM-judged string field direction_novelty ∈ {novel, incremental, repetitive} produced by audit_agents._run_master_plan_audit (master_plan_audit.md) and validated against output_schema (output_schema.py:116). It is advisory only. Stagnation detection (stagnation_analyzer._analyze_stagnation) is also LLM-judged and gated behind a data-sufficiency check (opp_coverage>=0.8).

SPOT VERIFICATION (spot_analyzer.py): parse_diff → generate_test_scenarios (heuristic per-function-name scenarios) → run_bot_scenario (subprocess synthetic request) → verify_behavior (action vs expected_behavior). Not wired into the live pipeline as a gate in the audited files — verify_behavior is a deterministic comparator; the LLM spot_analyzer prompt (spot_analyzer.md) is a separate optional check.

**优点**：

- Batched single-merge design in battle_experience correctly avoids the parallel read-modify-write data-loss bug — all per-match summaries are concatenated and merged into the live file in ONE LLM call (battle_experience.py:300-301, 352-388), preserving chaining semantics and cutting cost ~N:1 (battle_experience.py:355-359).
- EXHAUSTED-direction gate is engineered defensively across multiple components: tolerant regex marker matching (handles '[POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED]' / '[EXHAUSTED]' / '[EXHAUSTED — hard gate]' variants) in experience_archivist._tag_identity (experience_archivist.py:22), agent_workers._extract_exhausted_block (agent_workers.py:82), and tool_planning._extract_exhausted_keywords (tool_planning.py:589) — closes a prior round-trip bug where an LLM-appended suffix silently disabled the gate.
- Tag-identity round-trip gate aborts the consolidator write if an EXHAUSTED tag class would vanish entirely (experience_archivist.py:123-138), with a logged system event — prevents consolidation from silently neutering the exhausted-direction gate.
- Two-tier EXHAUSTED policy (RECENT_LESSONS = hard worker ban, other sections = advisory) lets old exhaustion expire naturally instead of permanently blacklisting directions (agent_workers.py:56-118, experience_consolidator.md:29-34) — a principled exploration/exploitation balance.
- Stagnation analysis refuses to judge when data is insufficient (opp_coverage<0.8 returns 'continue, low confidence' — stagnation_analyzer.py:36-43), and distinguishes LLM-infra failure from a business 'no stagnation' verdict via the llm_failed marker (stagnation_analyzer.py:172-185) so callers don't act on infra guesses.
- Reap protects sample-starved variance victims (rd>100 or games<600) and only relaxes protection past the hard cap (tool_bot_management.py:64-75) — prevents reaping bots that are bad merely because they haven't been evaluated enough.
- Fix injection is idempotent (guard strings) and applied at all four code-copy sites (prepare_next_gen, worker retry, seed, crossover) so known correctness bugs can't silently regress when a worker or crossover edits the relevant file (fix_injection.py:147, applied from tool_gates.py:370, tool_planning.py:954, code_verification.py:207, agent_review.py:315).
- Daemon match selection uses 60/40 exploration-under-eval vs diversity with a count_penalty decay + new-pair bonus + shuffle (elo_daemon.py:220-229) — a non-trivial, documented diversity policy rather than pure round-robin.
- battle_experience per-match failures are retryable (fail_count 1-2) with 3-strike force-skip poison-pill (battle_experience.py:141-150, 366-369) so a single corrupt replay or transient LLM failure doesn't permanently block the queue or silently drop data.

**缺陷/瓶颈**（含 file:line 证据 + 严重度）：

- [HIGH] trim_experience_pool is a naive line-count-only decay: it keeps the last 100 lines and drops the oldest, with zero awareness of section structure, EXHAUSTED tags, version freshness, or measured EV. A still-valid lesson at the top of OPPONENT_MODELING can be silently destroyed while a stale contradictory one lower survives. This is memory decay by byte position, not by value. — *证据：experience_pool.py:33-38 (keeps last 100 lines, drops oldest, no semantic/time logic); called from tool_bot_management.py:217 and evolution_infra re-export*
- [HIGH] battle_experience.md has NO mechanical decay or cap at all — it relies entirely on the LLM honoring the prompt's 'keep under 80 lines' instruction. Over 100+ generations of incremental single-match merges, the file drifts: the LLM self-trims inconsistently and there is no programmatic backstop. The two memory stores have asymmetric lifecycle policies for no principled reason. — *证据：battle_experience.py:597 (get_battle_experience just reads file, no cap); battle_experience_update.md:14 ('Keep total output under 80 lines' is an LLM self-instruction only); no trim_experience_pool equivalent called anywhere for battle_experience (grep shows zero call sites)*
- [HIGH] Novelty is never measured mechanically — there is no quantitative reuse/novelty metric anywhere in the subsystem. The only novelty signal is an LLM-judged string. The framework's stated failure modes (planning-loop repetition, worker zero-changes, diversity collapse) are all symptoms of missing mechanical novelty tracking. Reuse of the same direction can only be caught by LLM judgment, which historically under-detects (the code comments repeatedly note 'direction_audit under-detected v82 repetition'). — *证据：No code reference found; direction_novelty only emitted as LLM string at audit_agents.py:47 and master_plan_audit.md:48; reuse measured nowhere; the only mechanical reuse signal is the EXHAUSTED tag which is itself LLM-assigned by the consolidator (experience_consolidator.md:26-37)*
- [MED] Reap protection threshold (rd>100) combined with rd regrowth (daemon decay_rd inflates rd for idle bots) creates a protection bias toward old idle bots, and the conservative-rating sort (r-2*rd) systematically penalizes exactly those rd-recovered veterans. The two mechanisms fight: protection saves them, but when protection is bypassed (pool>33) the r-2*rd sort culls them preferentially. This is a documented latent instability (the NOTE at tool_bot_management.py:59-63 acknowledges the tension) that can cause batch-misclassification of anchor bots. — *证据：tool_bot_management.py:67 (rd>100 protect), :80 (sort by r-2*rd), :116 (event message says 'by H2H average win rate' but selection is r-2*rd); reapweakest MCP tool description tool_bot_management.py:132 says 'cull the weakest bot by H2H average win rate'*
- [HIGH] Fix injection registry carries a dead template (BOT-002b active=False) and the search strings are exact-match brittle to source drift. Once a worker edits the surrounding lines (e.g. reformats card_utils.py), the BOT-001a wheel-straight search no longer matches and the correctness fix silently stops applying — recorded only as a log warning, not a gate failure. Over 100+ generations of worker edits this is a likely-silent correctness regression source. — *证据：fix_injection.py:151-156 (search not found → warn+continue, fix effectively skipped with no failure propagation); applied at 4 sites but search drift is silent (tool_gates.py:371, tool_planning.py:955, code_verification.py:208, agent_review.py:316); log_fix_application only emits warn severity if 'skipped and applied' (fix_injection.py:186) — a fully-skipped fix logs only 'info'*
- [MED] experience_pool.md and battle_experience.md are two separate memory stores both injected into the Master prompt with overlapping but divergent schema and lifecycle. experience_pool is consolidated every 3 gens with section enforcement; battle_experience is merged every match with a different section schema. The Master reads both with no de-duplication or conflict resolution between them — a lesson can be stated as ADVISORY in one and as a hard STRENGTH PATTERN in the other. This is a memory-fragmentation / drift risk. — *证据：master_prompt.md:13 (experience_pool.md injected) and master_prompt.md:134 ({battle_experience} injected); both are LLM-curated markdown feeding the same Master with overlapping remit (battle_experience_update.md 'STRENGTH PATTERNS/VERSION TRENDS/ACTIONABLE INSIGHTS' vs experience_pool.md 'OPPONENT_MODELING/POSTFLOP_STRATEGY/...')*
- [MED] battle_experience random-samples matches from the unanalyzed pool (battle_experience.py:204-210), which defeats deterministic accumulation order but does not actually ensure recency balance — a backlog of old unanalyzed matches can be perpetually under-sampled while new ones keep arriving, or conversely a flood of new matches starves old ones. There is no coverage-targeted selection (unlike the daemon's pick_matches which has under-eval weighting). — *证据：battle_experience.py:204-209 (random.sample from candidates); :153 docstring claims 'Random-samples from the candidate pool to avoid recency bias, returns ... oldest first'*
- [HIGH] EXHAUSTED tagging of brand-new (v101) mechanisms contradicts the consolidator's documented intent ('If the same type of lesson appears for 3+ consecutive generations'). The live experience_pool.md tags v99/v100/v101 RECENT_LESSONS entries [POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED] even though they are single-generation new mechanisms — this is memory pollution: a fresh direction is pre-marked exhausted, which then flows into the worker forbidden_directions block as a hard ban. The consolidator LLM is over-tagging. — *证据：experience_pool.md RECENT_LESSONS (live file): v100 entry 'active risk: violates do NOT treat unknown opener as tight by default'; v101 entry 'fixed-ratio sizing IS the exhausted standalone sizing-tuning direction — must earn >=100g H2H proof before assuming success' tagged [POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED] while being the current gen's new mechanism*
- [MED] spot_analyzer.run_bot_scenario builds a synthetic protocol payload with arbitrary negative markers (-10, pot, to_call) that no real bot is documented to interpret, and uses a 10s subprocess timeout; the verify_behavior comparator is a trivial action-equality check that cannot tell whether a behavior change is the Master's intent. The deterministic verify_behavior path appears unused as a live gate (no call site found in the pipeline tool files audited). — *证据：spot_analyzer.py:237-272 (10s timeout, last-line parse, marker -10); :330-398 (verify_behavior simple comparator); no grep hit wiring run_bot_scenario/verify_behavior into tool_gates or quality_gates*
- [LOW] stagnation analyzer's generation-trend window is fixed at last-8 tags and lineage at source_v-5..source_v (stagnation_analyzer.py:51, :70) — a hardcoded window that cannot distinguish a genuine multi-generation plateau from normal exploratory noise, and the trend it computes is H2H winrate which is itself noisy at low game counts (the same problem the reap policy tries to mitigate). Two noisy signals feeding each other. — *证据：stagnation_analyzer.py:51 recent_tags = tags[-8:]; :70 range(max(1, source_v-5), source_v+1) — both fixed-size windows; no weighting by rating-magnitude or by whether a windowed bot was later reaped as a failed direction*

**Prompt 问题**：

- `web/core/prompts/experience_consolidator.md`：Consolidator only knows the [POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED] marker (verbatim) but the live pool already contains multiple markers ([POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED] and the code tolerates [EXHAUSTED — hard gate] variants via regex). The prompt insists on the EXACT literal marker and forbids suffixes, yet downstream code (experience_archivist._tag_identity, agent_workers._extract_exhausted_block, tool_planning._extract_exhausted_keywords) all use a tolerant regex. The prompt's 'do not add suffixes' instruction fights a downstream that is specifically engineered to tolerate them — an LLM following the prompt literally is fine, but the prompt is over-constraining and the tiering claim ('RECENT_LESSONS = hard ban, migrated = advisory') is asserted by the prompt but enforced only by a per-section state machine that depends on this same LLM reliably sorting lessons into the right section. → 建议：Drop the contradiction: tell the consolidator the marker MAY be '[POSSIBLY EXHAUSTED]' OR '[EXHAUSTED ...]' (regex-tolerated downstream) and that what matters is the SECTION it lands in, not the suffix. Make the RECENT_LESSONS-only-last-3-gens invariant an explicit, checked instruction (the tiering state machine hinges on it) and give a concrete 'move stale RECENT_LESSONS entries to their strategy section and downgrade to advisory' step instead of leaving it implicit.
- `web/core/prompts/master_plan_audit.md`：Novelty is asked of the LLM ('Compare against recent commit messages. If the last 3 commits all tried X, a 4th is unlikely to succeed') with no concrete data feed and no definition of the three labels beyond one-line glosses. The audit result is advisory and there is no mechanical novelty backstop anywhere in code, so a repetitive plan reworded slightly will score 'novel'. → 建议：Either inject the actual last-K commit subjects + the extracted EXHAUSTED keyword phrases into the audit prompt so the LLM compares against real text, and/or add a deterministic distinctive-token novelty score alongside the LLM verdict and ask the LLM to reconcile its label with that score. Make 'incremental' vs 'repetitive' depend on a concrete overlap threshold, not vibes.
- `web/core/prompts/master_prompt.md`：Plateau protocol tells the Master it MAY revisit an EXHAUSTED direction if combined with a NEW independent second mechanism and that EXHAUSTED entries are ADVISORY not bans — but the worker prompt (agent_workers._extract_exhausted_block) injects RECENT_LESSONS EXHAUSTED entries as a HARD 'Do NOT implement ... automatic rejection' ban. A Master that legitimately heeds the plateau protocol and proposes an EXHAUSTED+new-mechanism plan will have its workers auto-refuse the very direction the Master authorized. The two prompts express contradictory policy on the same EXHAUSTED entries. → 建议：Reconcile the two policies: either (a) make the worker hard-ban conditional on the Master plan NOT carrying an explicit 'EXHAUSTED+structural' exemption token, or (b) soften the worker <forbidden_directions> to advisory when the Master plan declares a structural companion mechanism. Document the exemption path in master_prompt.md so the Master knows how to lift the ban.
- `web/core/prompts/battle_experience_update.md`：Output is capped at 80 lines and each observation MUST cite specific versions + win rates, and the LLM must self-trim the weakest if it exceeds 80 lines — but the file has no programmatic backstop and the prompt does not tell the LLM the current line count. As the file approaches/exceeds 80 lines the LLM must infer length, and incremental single-match merges with no length context tend to drift over the cap (the live file is 35 lines now, but it has no decay path other than LLM self-discipline). → 建议：Inject the current line count and a 'must shed X lines' directive into the prompt when the file is near the cap, and/or add a programmatic trim (mirror experience_pool.py) so the store cannot grow unbounded regardless of LLM compliance. Tell the LLM explicitly which sections to compress first.
- `web/core/prompts/battle_experience_update.md`：No explicit anti-contradiction instruction and no instruction to remove an observation when current bot behavior no longer matches it. Rule 3 only removes observations contradicted by '>=3 newer matches' with explicit contradiction count — which the per-match summary rarely carries, so stale/contradicted observations accumulate. The merge examples show version-level contradiction but the per-match feed is a single match summary, not a contradiction-count ledger. → 建议：Add an instruction to actively retire observations whose cited version has been reaped or whose referenced behavior has been refactored away, and lower the contradiction threshold for removal (e.g. 'flag for removal after 1 clear contradiction, remove after 2'). Feed the current source bot's name into the prompt so the LLM can prune entries about dead versions.
